INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM_ALL ( DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID , CELL_MODEL, NICK_NAME, DMS_SALES, DMS_CUSTOMER, OWNER , DEPT_ID, DEPT_NAME_KO, FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT , ITEM_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_OWNER, BIZ_TYPE , SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS , ITEM_NAME, VMI_YN, CUST_GROUP, CUST_GROUP_DTL, APPLICATION, ROUTE , MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY, FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, CELL_TYPE , FORECAST_INFO_ALL, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC, FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION , CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE, MODULE_PER_CAR , SORT_ORDER_NO, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, PLANT_SITE_ID , ITEM_VALID_YN, END_CUSTOMER, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT ) SELECT F.DIVISION_ID , F.PROJECT_CODE , F.SCM_SITE_ID , F.ITEM_ID , F.ATTRIBUTE5 AS CELL_MODEL , T2.NICK_NAME , F.ATTRIBUTE1 AS DMS_SALES , F.ATTRIBUTE2 AS DMS_CUSTOMER , (SELECT LISTAGG(X.USER_NAME_EN, ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY X.USER_NAME_EN) FROM (SELECT DISTINCT ZA.USER_NAME_EN FROM (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(F.OWNER, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOK FROM DUAL CONNECT BY LEVEL <= REGEXP_COUNT(F.OWNER, ',') + 1) TK JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO ZA ON ZA.USER_ID = TK.TOK) X ) AS OWNER , TEAM.DEPT_ID , TEAM.DEPT_NAME_KO , CASE WHEN F.ITEM_ID = LOOKA.MODEL THEN TO_NUMBER(LOOKA.CELL_PACK_COUNT DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) ELSE T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT END AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT , T2.ITEM_TYPE , NVL(MP.PROJECT_NAME, F.PROJECT_CODE) AS PROJECT_NAME , J.USER_NAME_EN AS PROJECT_OWNER , '' AS BIZ_TYPE , S.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER , I.COUNTRY_NAME , H.CITY_NAME , '' AS PROJECT_STATUS , T2.ITEM_NAME , S.VMI_YN , F.CUST_GROUP , F.CUST_GROUP_DTL , F.APPLICATION , F.ROUTE , T2.MDM_PROJECT_NAME , TO_NUMBER(T2.MIN_CAPACITY DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS MIN_CAPACITY , CASE WHEN F.ITEM_ID = LOOKA.MODEL THEN LOOKA.BIZ_TYPE ELSE T2.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE END AS FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE , LOOK.NAME AS CELL_TYPE , T2.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC || T2.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION AS FORECAST_INFO_ALL , T2.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC , T2.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION , T4.NICK_NAME AS CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME , TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_CAR DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS CELL_PER_CAR , TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_MODULE DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS CELL_PER_MODULE , TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_CAR DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) / CASE WHEN NVL(TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_MODULE DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR), 0) = 0 THEN 1 ELSE TO_NUMBER(F.CELL_PER_MODULE DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) END AS MODULE_PER_CAR , NULL AS SORT_ORDER_NO , TO_CHAR(T2.ENERGE) AS ENERGE , TO_NUMBER(T2.CELL_QUANTITY DEFAULT NULL ON CONVERSION ERROR) AS CELL_QUANTITY , '-' AS PLANT_SITE_ID , F.VALID_YN AS ITEM_VALID_YN , S.END_CUSTOMER , NULL AS PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE_ITEM F INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V T2 ON F.DIVISION_ID = T2.DIVISION_ID AND F.ITEM_ID = T2.ITEM_ID INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SITE S ON F.DIVISION_ID = S.DIVISION_ID AND F.SCM_SITE_ID = S.SCM_SITE_ID AND S.SITE_TYPE = 'SALES' INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_CITY H ON S.CITY = H.CITY INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_COUNTRY I ON S.COUNTRY = I.COUNTRY LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_PROJECT MP ON F.DIVISION_ID = MP.DIVISION_ID AND F.PROJECT_CODE = MP.PROJECT_CODE LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO TEAM ON TEAM.DEPT_ID = F.DEPT_ID LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_USER_INFO J ON J.USER_ID = CASE WHEN INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') = 0 THEN F.OWNER ELSE SUBSTR(F.OWNER, 1, INSTR(F.OWNER, ',') - 1) END LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_MST_SSO_DEPT_INFO K ON K.DEPT_ID = F.DEPT_ID LEFT JOIN ( SELECT SEGMENT1 AS CODE, SEGMENT2 AS NAME FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL WHERE LOOKUP_TYPE = 'CELL_TYPE' ) LOOK ON LOOK.CODE = T2.CELL_TYPE LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MST_ITEM_SINGLE_V T4 ON T2.DIVISION_ID = T4.DIVISION_ID AND T2.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL = T4.ITEM_ID LEFT JOIN ( SELECT A.MODEL, A.BIZ_TYPE, A.CELL_PACK_COUNT, A.MODEL_RANK FROM ( SELECT SEGMENT2 AS MODEL , SEGMENT3 AS BIZ_TYPE , SEGMENT4 AS CELL_PACK_COUNT , RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY SE
LOOKUP_TYPE = 'DM_BIZ_TYPE_40' AND SEGMENT1 = '40' ) A WHERE MODEL_RANK = 1 ) LOOKA ON LOOKA.MODEL = F.ITEM_ID WHERE F.DIVISION_ID = :B1 

END OF STMT

INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_LAST_DATA_SETUP_FCST_C (DIVISION_ID, PROJECT_CODE, SCM_SITE_ID, ITEM_ID, DOM_EXP, FCST_TYPE, INCOTERMS, MEASURE, VERSION_NO, CREATE_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY) SELECT A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID , A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE, A.VERSION_NO , MAX(A.CREATION_DATE) AS CREATE_DATE , MAX(A.LAST_UPDATED_BY) AS LAST_UPDATED_BY FROM SCM_MGR.DM_PLN_MKT_FCST_C A INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_SITE_ITEM C ON C.DIVISION_ID = A.DIVISION_ID AND C.PROJECT_CODE = A.PROJECT_CODE AND C.SCM_SITE_ID = A.SCM_SITE_ID AND C.ITEM_ID = A.ITEM_ID INNER JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_T_VERSION V ON V.VERSION_NO = A.VERSION_NO WHERE A.DIVISION_ID = :B3 AND A.YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B1 GROUP BY A.DIVISION_ID, A.PROJECT_CODE, A.SCM_SITE_ID, A.ITEM_ID , A.DOM_EXP, A.FCST_TYPE, A.INCOTERMS, A.MEASURE, A.VERSION_NO

END OF STMT

PARSING IN CURSOR #140010324610992 len=1336 dep=1 uid=159 oct=2 lid=159 tim=1584352277625 hv=1712817624 ad='d77c9b10' sqlid='fsb8nn1m1g1fs'

INSERT INTO SCM_MGR.GTT_TEMP_INTO_GROUP_MEASURE ( USER_GROUP, DIVISION_ID, MEASURE, DAYS, FROM_TIME, TO_TIME, LIMIT_MEASURE ) SELECT B.USER_GROUP, B.DIVISION_ID, B.MEASURE, B.DAYS, B.FROM_TIME, B.TO_TIME, LM.TOKEN AS LIMIT_MEASURE FROM (SELECT A.SEGMENT1 AS USER_GROUP, A.SEGMENT3 AS DIVISION_ID, A.SEGMENT4 AS MEASURE, DT.TOKEN AS DAYS , A.ATTRIBUTE2 AS FROM_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE3 AS TO_TIME, A.ATTRIBUTE4 AS LIMIT_MEASURE FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL A CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(A.ATTRIBUTE1, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN FROM DUAL CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(A.ATTRIBUTE1, ','), 0) + 1) DT WHERE A.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'GET_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE' AND A.VALID_YN = 'Y' AND (A.SEGMENT1 = :B3 OR A.SEGMENT1 IN (SELECT G.USER_GR_ID FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_USER_GR_MPG_M G WHERE G.USER_ID IN (:B3 , :B4 ) AND G.USE_YN = 'Y')) AND A.SEGMENT2 = :B2 AND A.SEGMENT3 = :B1 ) B CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT REGEXP_SUBSTR(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, '[^,]+', 1, LEVEL) AS TOKEN FROM DUAL CONNECT BY LEVEL <= NVL(REGEXP_COUNT(B.LIMIT_MEASURE, ','), 0) + 1) LM WHERE (REPLACE(B.DAYS, ' ', '') IS NULL OR B.DAYS = TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'DY', 'NLS_DATE_LANGUAGE=ENGLISH')) AND (REPLACE(B.FROM_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL OR B.FROM_TIME <= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI')) AND (REPLACE(B.TO_TIME, ' ', '') IS NULL OR B.TO_TIME >= TO_CHAR(SYSDATE, 'HH24MI'))

END OF STMT

BUCKET_KEY, QTY, MEASURE_GROUP , CELL_MODEL, NICK_NAME, DMS_SALES, DMS_CUSTOMER, OWNER, DEPT_ID, DEPT_NAME_KO , FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT, ITEM_TYPE, PROJECT_NAME, PROJECT_OWNER, BIZ_TYPE , SOLDTO_CUSTOMER, COUNTRY_NAME, CITY_NAME, PROJECT_STATUS, ITEM_NAME, VMI_YN , CUST_GROUP, CUST_GROUP_DTL, APPLICATION, ROUTE, MDM_PROJECT_NAME, MIN_CAPACITY , FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE, CELL_TYPE, FORECAST_INFO_ALL, FORECAST_INFO_SPEC , FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION, CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME, CELL_PER_CAR, CELL_PER_MODULE , MODULE_PER_CAR, SORT_ORDER_NO, ENERGE, CELL_QUANTITY, AS_FROM_SITE , CREATE_DATE, LAST_UPDATED_BY, SENDER_CODE, SENDER_NAME, PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT , CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD, NOTATION, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, GROUP_ID , SUN, MON, TUE, WED, THU, FRI, SAT , YYYYMM, YYYYMMWW, WEEK_END_YN, MONTH_END_YN , NEW_FLAG, AS_YN) SELECT F.DIVISION_ID, F.PROJECT_CODE, F.END_CUSTOMER, F.SCM_SITE_ID, F.ITEM_ID , F.DOM_EXP, F.FCST_TYPE, F.INCOTERMS, F.MEASURE, F.PLANT_SITE_ID , F.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID , CAL.BUCKET_TYPE , CAL.BUCKET_KEY , SUM(F.QTY) AS QTY , MAX(F.MEASURE_GROUP) , MAX(F.CELL_MODEL), MAX(F.NICK_NAME), MAX(F.DMS_SALES), MAX(F.DMS_CUSTOMER) , MAX(F.OWNER), MAX(F.DEPT_ID), MAX(F.DEPT_NAME_KO) , MAX(F.FORECAST_INFO_CELL_PACK_COUNT), MAX(F.ITEM_TYPE) , MAX(F.PROJECT_NAME), MAX(F.PROJECT_OWNER), MAX(F.BIZ_TYPE) , MAX(F.SOLDTO_CUSTOMER), MAX(F.COUNTRY_NAME), MAX(F.CITY_NAME) , MAX(F.PROJECT_STATUS), MAX(F.ITEM_NAME), MAX(F.VMI_YN) , MAX(F.CUST_GROUP), MAX(F.CUST_GROUP_DTL), MAX(F.APPLICATION) , MAX(F.ROUTE), MAX(F.MDM_PROJECT_NAME), MAX(F.MIN_CAPACITY) , MAX(F.FORECAST_INFO_BIZ_TYPE), MAX(F.CELL_TYPE) , MAX(F.FORECAST_INFO_ALL), MAX(F.FORECAST_INFO_SPEC) , MAX(F.FORECAST_INFOR_VERSION), MAX(F.CELL_MODEL_NICK_NAME) , MAX(F.CELL_PER_CAR), MAX(F.CELL_PER_MODULE), MAX(F.MODULE_PER_CAR) , MAX(F.SORT_ORDER_NO), MAX(F.ENERGE), MAX(F.CELL_QUANTITY) , NVL(MAX(F.AS_FROM_SITE), ' '), MAX(F.CREATE_DATE), MAX(F.LAST_UPDATED_BY) , MAX(F.SENDER_CODE), MAX(F.SENDER_NAME), MAX(F.PROJECT_S_OP_REPORT) , MAX(F.CURRENCY), MAX(F.VERSION_NO), MAX(F.CUTOFF_YYYYMMDD) , MAX(F.NOTATION) , CASE WHEN :B5 = 'ADMIN' THEN 'Y' WHEN EXISTS ( SELECT 1 FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_ADMIN_GROUP_MEASURE MA WHERE MA.MEASURE = F.MEASURE AND MA.EDIT_AUTH_YN = 'Y' AND (MA.TODAY_LIMIT_YN = 'N' OR (MA.TODAY_LIMIT_YN = 'Y' AND INSTR( REPLACE(TRIM(MA.TODAY_DAY_LIST),' ',''), CASE TO_NUMBER(TO_CHAR(SYSDATE,'D')) WHEN 1 THEN 'SUN' WHEN 2 THEN 'MON' WHEN 3 THEN 'TUE' WHEN 4 THEN 'WED' WHEN 5 THEN 'THU' WHEN 6 THEN 'FRI' WHEN 7 THEN 'SAT' END) > 0)) ) THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS EDIT_FLAG , 999 AS SORT_KEY , NULL AS GROUP_ID , PVT.SUN, PVT.MON, PVT.TUE, PVT.WED, PVT.THU, PVT.FRI, PVT.SAT , CAL.YYYYMM, CAL.YYYYMMWW, CAL.WEEK_END_YN, CAL.MONTH_END_YN , MAX('N') AS NEW_FLAG , MAX(CASE WHEN :B4 IS NULL OR :B4 = 'ALL' THEN 'N' WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM (SELECT TOKEN FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_PARAM_TOKEN WHERE PARAM_NM = 'p_scm_site_id') SA JOIN SCM_MGR.SCM_ADM_LOOKUP_MASTER_DETAIL SB ON SA.TOKEN = SB.SEGMENT1 AND SB.LOOKUP_TYPE = 'AS_SITE_PLANT') THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END) AS AS_YN FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_TEMP_FCST_N_PRICE F INNER JOIN ( SELECT D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD , D.BASIS_YYYYMM || D.ISO_WEEK AS WEEK_KEY , CASE WHEN D.BASIS_YYYYMM || D.ISO_WEEK = WE.ISO_WEEK || WE.BASIS_YYYYMM THEN WE.WEEK_END_DATE ELSE D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD END AS BUCKET_TYPE_KEY , TO_CHAR(D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS BUCKET_KEY , 'D' AS BUCKET_TYPE , D.BASIS_YYYYMM AS YYYYMM , D.BASIS_YYYYMM || LPAD(TRIM(D.ISO_WEEK), 2, '0') AS YYYYMMWW , CASE WHEN WE2.WEEK_END_DATE IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS WEEK_END_YN , CASE WHEN ME.MONTH_END_DATE IS NOT NULL THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS MONTH_END_YN FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M D LEFT JOIN SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_CAL_WEEK_END_DATE WE ON WE.ISO_WEEK = D.ISO_WEEK AND WE.BASIS_YYYYMM = D.BASIS_YYYYMM LEFT JOIN (SELECT 
N :B2 AND :B1 GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM, ISO_WEEK) WE2 ON WE2.WEEK_END_DATE = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD LEFT JOIN (SELECT MAX(BASIS_YYYYMMDD) AS MONTH_END_DATE FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M WHERE BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B1 GROUP BY BASIS_YYYYMM) ME ON ME.MONTH_END_DATE = D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD WHERE D.BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B3 ) CAL ON CAL.BASIS_YYYYMMDD = F.YYYYMMDD LEFT JOIN ( SELECT BC.DIVISION_ID, BC.SCM_SITE_ID, BC.INCOTERMS , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'SUN' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS SUN , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'MON' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS MON , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'TUE' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS TUE , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'WED' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS WED , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'THU' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS THU , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'FRI' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS FRI , MAX(CASE WHEN BC.YYYYMMDD = 'SAT' THEN CASE WHEN BC.BUCKET_PER = 'Y' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END END) AS SAT FROM SCM_MGR.DM_MST_BUCKET_CONVERSION BC GROUP BY BC.DIVISION_ID, BC.SCM_SITE_ID, BC.INCOTERMS ) PVT ON PVT.DIVISION_ID = F.DIVISION_ID AND PVT.SCM_SITE_ID = F.SCM_SITE_ID AND PVT.INCOTERMS = F.INCOTERMS GROUP BY F.DIVISION_ID, F.PROJECT_CODE, F.END_CUSTOMER, F.SCM_SITE_ID, F.ITEM_ID , F.DOM_EXP, F.FCST_TYPE, F.INCOTERMS, F.MEASURE, F.PLANT_SITE_ID , F.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, CAL.BUCKET_TYPE, CAL.BUCKET_KEY, F.MEASURE_GROUP , F.SORT_ORDER_NO , PVT.SUN, PVT.MON, PVT.TUE, PVT.WED, PVT.THU, PVT.FRI, PVT.SAT , CAL.YYYYMM, CAL.YYYYMMWW, CAL.WEEK_END_YN, CAL.MONTH_END_YN

END OF STMT

MERGE INTO SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP T USING ( SELECT NVL(DIVISION_ID, '~') AS DIVISION_ID , NVL(PROJECT_CODE, '~') AS PROJECT_CODE , NVL(END_CUSTOMER, '~') AS END_CUSTOMER , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '~') AS SCM_SITE_ID , NVL(ITEM_ID, '~') AS ITEM_ID , NVL(DOM_EXP, '~') AS DOM_EXP , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '~') AS FCST_TYPE , NVL(INCOTERMS, '~') AS INCOTERMS , NVL(PLANT_SITE_ID, '~') AS PLANT_SITE_ID , NVL(MEASURE, '~') AS MEASURE , MAX(CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) KEEP (DENSE_RANK LAST ORDER BY CASE WHEN BUCKET_KEY >= :B1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END , BUCKET_KEY) AS REP_CELL_PROD FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP WHERE CELL_PROD_SITE_ID IS NOT NULL GROUP BY NVL(DIVISION_ID, '~') , NVL(PROJECT_CODE, '~') , NVL(END_CUSTOMER, '~') , NVL(SCM_SITE_ID, '~') , NVL(ITEM_ID, '~') , NVL(DOM_EXP, '~') , NVL(FCST_TYPE, '~') , NVL(INCOTERMS, '~') , NVL(PLANT_SITE_ID, '~') , NVL(MEASURE, '~') HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT CELL_PROD_SITE_ID) > 1 ) S ON ( NVL(T.DIVISION_ID, '~') = S.DIVISION_ID AND NVL(T.PROJECT_CODE, '~') = S.PROJECT_CODE AND NVL(T.END_CUSTOMER, '~') = S.END_CUSTOMER AND NVL(T.SCM_SITE_ID, '~') = S.SCM_SITE_ID AND NVL(T.ITEM_ID, '~') = S.ITEM_ID AND NVL(T.DOM_EXP, '~') = S.DOM_EXP AND NVL(T.FCST_TYPE, '~') = S.FCST_TYPE AND NVL(T.INCOTERMS, '~') = S.INCOTERMS AND NVL(T.PLANT_SITE_ID, '~') = S.PLANT_SITE_ID AND NVL(T.MEASURE, '~') = S.MEASURE ) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID = S.REP_CELL_PROD WHERE NVL(T.CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, '~') <> S.REP_CELL_PROD

END OF STMT

        WHERE MEASURE IN ('KRW','USD','LOC')

         GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, PROJECT_CODE, AS_FROM_SITE, PLANT_SITE_ID, TEAM_ID, MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_ORDER_NO, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, NOTATION, YYYYMM, MONTH_END_YN, MEASURE

        UNION ALL

        SELECT DIVISION_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, PROJECT_CODE, AS_FROM_SITE, PLANT_SITE_ID, TEAM_ID, MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, NOTATION, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, SORT_ORDER_NO, 'M_' || YYYYMM AS PIVOT_BUCKET, AVG(QTY) AS QTY
        FROM ( SELECT MSP.*, MSP.DEPT_ID AS TEAM_ID, MSP.DEPT_NAME_KO AS TEAM_NAME_KO /*[UICATEGORY 2026-08-07]*/, MSP.CELL_MODEL AS FORECAST_INFO_CELL_MODEL, NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.CREATE_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD HH24:MI:SS'), ' ') AS LAST_UPDATE_DATE, NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.SUN),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.MON),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.TUE),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.WED),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.THU),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.FRI),'0')||NVL(TO_CHAR(MSP.SAT),'0') AS BUCKET FROM SCM_MGR.GT_DMF0082_MF_SP MSP ) F
        WHERE MEASURE IN ('KRW','USD','LOC')
        AND ((MEASURE NOT IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP') AND QTY <> 0) OR MEASURE IN ('SCM_FCST','FCST_GAP'))
         GROUP BY DIVISION_ID, END_CUSTOMER, SCM_SITE_ID, GROUP_ID, ITEM_ID, NICK_NAME, INCOTERMS, FCST_TYPE, DOM_EXP, CURRENCY, VERSION_NO, PROJECT_CODE, AS_FROM_SITE, PLANT_SITE_ID, TEAM_ID, MEASURE, MEASURE_GROUP, BUCKET, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_KEY, NEW_FLAG, ATTR1, VMI_STD_DIO, AS_YN, EDIT_FLAG, SORT_ORDER_NO, CELL_PROD_SITE_ID, NOTATION, YYYYMM
    ) SRC

SELECT A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD , A.BASIS_YYYYMM , CASE WHEN SUBSTR(A.BASIS_START_DATE, -2) = '01' THEN 'Y' ELSE 'N' END AS FIRST_WEEK_FLAG , CASE WHEN SUBSTR(TO_CHAR(TO_DATE(A.BASIS_START_DATE,'YYYYMMDD')+7,'YYYYMMDD'),1,6) = SUBSTR(A.BASIS_START_DATE,1,6) THEN 'N' ELSE 'Y' END AS END_MONTH_FLAG , COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY A.BASIS_YYYYMM) AS MONTH_DAY_CNT , COUNT(*) OVER (PARTITION BY A.ISO_WEEK) AS WEEK_DAY_CNT , A.ISO_WEEK FROM SCM_MGR.SCM_SYS_CALENDAR_M A WHERE A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD BETWEEN :B2 AND :B1 ORDER BY A.BASIS_YYYYMMDD

END OF STMT
